## 禁区
tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs、tests/golden/*、tests/caves-checkpoint.test.ts、src/world/gen/WorldGen.ts。金标不再生。

## 背景
- 树 = pass #82 Planting Trees（cs:20085：GrowEpicTree + AddTrees cs:15642 + GrowTree cs:14171 + RandomizeTreeStyle）；我们在 TreePass.ts（此前含 randomizeTreeStyle 与栽树）。历史上修过半砖/坡面基座检查、活树链（192 vs 191 已修）——但**普通树的数量差**从未专项对过。
- 原版真值：tests/golden/wld/12345世界.wld 等 5 个客户端金标 + /tmp/vserver/*.wld 38 个服务器世界（任意种子可现生成，方法见记忆/文档：TerrariaServer -seed，SW_EVIL 对照可用 /tmp/vserver/evil_srv）。树在最终存档里可数（tile 5 树干族+活木 590 族）。
- rig（tools/_contentparity.test.ts）的 [Trees] 边界格子差不显眼≠树数相等——树是稀疏大对象，本专项以**逐棵计数+逐棵坐标**为准。

## 任务
1. **定量**：种子 12345（用户观察）+9293480：我们的完整生成 vs 原版 .wld，统计树干列数/树种分布（treeStyle 四区）/活木树数/棕榈数，差多少棵、差在哪些区。
2. **隔离定位**：树 pass 的输入（草面/表面状态）在管线里已验证位级正确（terrain→grass 全零），所以数量差大概率在 TreePass 自身或其前置登记（surface 数组/树基座门）。隔离重放：用原版 Trees 前置 dump 直构输入跑 TreePass，对拍 Trees dump 的树干格——归零=级联（找上游），不归零=TreePass 自因。
3. **自因则猎杀**：照用方法论（流指纹定标 tools/_findn.test.ts 族 + 通用陷阱清单：框架耗掷/GrowTree 链里的 CheckAndAdjust/半砖坡面门/for 条件重掷/id 空间）。已知 GrowTree 基座门很复杂（IsTileTypeFitForTree 族/邻格液体/三列净空）——重点查这些门的谓词近似。
4. 修复→双种子复验（计数相等+隔离四通道 0）→rig [Trees] 边界复验→报告追加 docs/worldgen/content-parity-vs-vanilla-2026-08-16.md + oracle 同步清单。

## 纪律
原版源码唯一权威（Terarria1456 WorldGen.cs 对应行号；可疑时 ~/.dotnet/tools/ilspycmd 对真 exe）；单次工具调用<8分钟；每段落盘；探针只读不掷。
```

117	    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;
118	    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);
119	    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);
120	  };
121	  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
122	  const height = rng.int(5, 16);                       // Next(5,17)
123	  let clear = height + 4;                              // num3
124	  if (JUNGLE_GRASS.has(st.type[gi])) clear += 5;
125	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - clear, index1 - 1)) return false;
126	  return growTrunk(st, rng, gs, i, index1, height);
127	}
128	
129	/** GrowEpicTree（L11850）：巨型树。基座严格草(2)、邻格草族、净空 55、height=Next(20,30) */
130	function growEpicTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, y: number): boolean {
131	  let index1 = y;
132	  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
133	  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
134	  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
135	  const GRASS = idsByVanillaSheet(2);
136	  // 基座整砖检查(GrowEpicTree 同 GrowTree 头部语义:半砖/坡面拒绝)
137	  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !GRASS.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
138	  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
139	  const family = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 109);
140	  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)
141	    && st.flags[st.idx(i + dx, index1)] && family.has(st.type[st.idx(i + dx, index1)]);
142	  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
143	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - 55, index1 - 1)) return false;
144	  return growTrunk(st, rng, gs, i, index1, rng.int(20, 29)); // Next(20,30)
145	}
146	
147	/** GrowPalmTree（L11785）：沙滩棕榈，干身倾斜帧 fy=num4（±2 步进趋向 num3） */
148	/** 1456 GrowPalmTree(cs:27270-27340)1:1;导出供 StructuresPass 绿洲消费段复用(2026-08-13 去重) */
149	export function growPalmTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, y: number): boolean {
150	  if (!st.inBounds(i, y)) return false;
151	  let index1 = y;
152	  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
153	  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
154	  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
155	  // 基座整砖检查(GrowPalmTree:27280-27285 active && !halfBrick && slope==0)
156	  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !PALM_GROUND.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
157	  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
158	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i, i, index1 - 2, index1 - 1)) return false;
159	  // 1456 终态(cs:27302):侧净空 num-num2 = index1-20(1405 :11800 是 index1-30,已换)
160	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 1, i + 1, index1 - 20, index1 - 3)) return false;
161	  const height = rng.int(10, 20);                 // Next(10,21)
162	  const target = rng.int(-8, 8) * 2;              // Next(-8,9)*2
163	  let tilt = 0;
164	  for (let k = 0; k < height; k++) {
165	    if (k === 0) {
166	      st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, PALM, 66, 0);
167	      continue;
168	    }
169	    if (k === height - 1) {
170	      st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(4, 6), tilt); // 顶段 fx=22*Next(4,7)
171	      continue;
172	    }
173	    if (tilt !== target) {
174	      const prog = k / height;                    // 倾斜步进阈值（L11814-11820）
175	      if (prog >= 0.25 && ((prog < 0.5 && rng.int(0, 12) === 0)
176	        || (prog < 0.7 && rng.int(0, 8) === 0)
177	        || (prog < 0.95 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) || true)) {
178	        tilt += Math.sign(target) * 2;
179	      }
180	    }
181	    st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(0, 2), tilt); // fx=22*Next(0,3)
182	  }
183	  return true;
184	}
185	
186	/** 湖畔棕榈水盒段（CactusPalmTreesAndCoral 中段 flag 分支，cs:21257-21273）：
187	 *  沙面锚点 (x,y) 的 50×25 水盒（[x-50,x+50)×[y-25,y+25)，InWorld 内液量求和，
188	 *  常量 cactusWaterWidth/Height/Limit = 50/25/25，cs:4258-4262）内液量和 /255 > 25
189	 *  时掷 Next(4)==0 → GrowPalmTree（TryGrowingTreeByType(323) → cs:30508-30509）。
190	 *  返回 true = 水盒超限（Next(4) 已掷，可能已种棕榈）→ 仙人掌段不再 PlantCactus；
191	 *  返回 false = 水盒未超限 → 由调用方走 PlantCactus（cs:21274）。 */
192	export function waterBoxPalmCheck(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): boolean {
193	  let sum = 0;
194	  for (let k = x - 50; k < x + 50; k++) {
195	    for (let l = y - 25; l < y + 25; l++) {
196	      if (st.inBounds(k, l)) sum += st.liquid[st.idx(k, l)];
197	    }
198	  }
199	  if (Math.trunc(sum / 255) > 25) {
200	    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
201	    return true;
202	  }
203	  return false;
204	}
205	
206	// ===================== GrowTreeWithSettings 族（宝石树 + 观赏树柳/樱） =====================
207	// ShimmerMakeBiome 的 aether 树走 TryGrowingTreeByType(583-589)（WorldGen.cs:30500-30542 →
208	// GrowTreeWithSettings 30546-30967）；AddTrees 的 1/20 观赏树走 616 柳 / 596 樱（cs:31558-31566）。
209	// 干身帧表与 GrowTree 完全同构（同一 switch，cs:30604-30700），复用 TRUNK。
210	
211	/** GrowTreeSettings profile（cs:3795-3886）：宝石树七种 + 观赏树柳/樱共用同一骨架，
212	 *  差异只在 GroundTest/WallTest/SaplingTileType/TreeTileType（高度均 7-12、Padding 4） */
213	export interface TreeProfile {
214	  treeTile: number;
215	  sapling: number;
216	  ground: Set<number>;   // GroundTest 谓词集
217	  walls: Set<number>;    // WallTest 谓词集
218	  heightMin: number;
219	  heightMax: number;     // 含端（Next(min, max+1)）
220	  topPadding: number;    // TreeTopPaddingNeeded
221	}
222	
223	/** GemTreeGroundTest（cs:30461）：Conversion.Stone{1,25,117,203} ∪ Moss */
224	const GEM_GROUND = idsByVanillaSheet(1, 25, 117, 203, 182, 180, 179, 381, 183, 181, 534, 536, 539, 625, 627);
225	/** IsTileTypeFitForTree 全集（cs:29852，含 633/661/662——基座根部 tuft 的适树判定） */
226	const GEM_TREE_FIT = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 70, 109, 147, 199, 477, 492, 633, 661, 662);
227	/** GemTreeWallTest（cs:30426-30458）= DefaultTreeWallTest(AllowsPlantsToGrow) ∪ 宝石墙补充表 */
228	const GEM_WALLS = new Set([
229	  0, 150, 138, 145, 107, 152, 140, 139, 141, 106, 245, 315, 317, 63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 81, 70, 264, 268, 265, 74, 80,
230	  2, 54, 55, 56, 57, 58, 59, 61, 185, 196, 197, 198, 199, 208, 209, 210, 211, 212, 213, 214, 215,
231	]);
232	/** DefaultTreeWallTest（cs:30417）= WallID.Sets.AllowsPlantsToGrow（WallID.cs:58） */
233	const DEFAULT_TREE_WALLS = new Set<number>([
234	  0, 150, 138, 145, 107, 152, 140, 139, 141, 106, 245, 315, 317, 63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 81, 70, 264, 268, 265, 74, 80,
235	]);
236	/** VanityTreeGroundTest（cs:30474）：Conversion.Grass{2,23,199,109,477,492} 去掉 23/199 */
237	const VANITY_GROUND = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 109, 477, 492);
238	/** EmptyTileCheck ignoreID=20（树苗族门）：活跃格仅容忍树族 tile 残留（cs:31611-31683） */
239	const GEM_EMPTY_ALLOW = idsByVanillaSheet(3, 24, 32, 61, 62, 69, 71, 73, 74, 82, 83, 84, 110, 113, 184, 201, 233, 352, 485, 529, 530, 637, 655);
240	
241	const GEM_PROFILE: TreeProfile = {
242	  treeTile: -1, sapling: GEM_SAPLING, ground: GEM_GROUND, walls: GEM_WALLS,
243	  heightMin: 7, heightMax: 12, topPadding: 4,
244	};
245	const SAKURA_PROFILE: TreeProfile = {
246	  treeTile: TILE_BY_KEY['v_596_vanity_tree_sakura']!,
247	  sapling: TILE_BY_KEY['v_595_sakura_sapling']!,
248	  ground: VANITY_GROUND, walls: DEFAULT_TREE_WALLS,
249	  heightMin: 7, heightMax: 12, topPadding: 4,
250	};
251	const WILLOW_PROFILE: TreeProfile = {
252	  treeTile: TILE_BY_KEY['v_616_vanity_tree_yellow_willow']!,
253	  sapling: TILE_BY_KEY['v_615_yellow_willow_sapling']!,
254	  ground: VANITY_GROUND, walls: DEFAULT_TREE_WALLS,
255	  heightMin: 7, heightMax: 12, topPadding: 4,
256	};
257	/** AshTreeGroundTest（cs:30487-30501）：仅灰烬草 633 */
258	const ASH_GROUND = idsByVanillaSheet(633);
259	const ASH_PROFILE: TreeProfile = {
260	  treeTile: TILE_BY_KEY['v_634_ash_tree']!,
261	  sapling: GEM_SAPLING,   // Tree_Ash.SaplingTileType=20（cs:3901；growTreeWithSettings 不消费）
262	  ground: ASH_GROUND, walls: DEFAULT_TREE_WALLS,
263	  heightMin: 7, heightMax: 12, topPadding: 4,   // Tree_Ash 高度 7-12 / Padding 4（cs:3898-3901）
264	};
265	
266	/** GrowTreeWithSettings（cs:30546-30967）1:1：宝石树/观赏树共用骨架。
267	 *  干身帧表与 GrowTree 同构（同一 switch，cs:30604-30700），复用 TRUNK。 */
268	export function growTreeWithSettings(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, p: TreeProfile): boolean {
269	
270	  // 越过树苗找地面行（cs:30547-30550）
271	  let num = y;
272	  while (st.inBounds(x, num) && st.flags[st.idx(x, num)] && st.type[st.idx(x, num)] === p.sapling) num++;
273	  if (!st.inBounds(x, num) || num < 1) return false;
274	  // 基座上一格三列无液体（cs:30551-30554）
275	  if (st.liquid[st.idx(x - 1, num - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x, num - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x + 1, num - 1)] !== 0) return false;
276	  // 基座整砖 + 墙白名单 + GroundTest（cs:30555-30566）
277	  const gi = st.idx(x, num);
278	  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0) return false;
279	  if (!p.walls.has(st.wall[st.idx(x, num - 1)])) return false;
280	  if (!p.ground.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
281	  // 两侧至少一侧同基座（cs:30568-30571）
282	  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(x + dx, num)
283	    && st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, num)] && p.ground.has(st.type[st.idx(x + dx, num)]);
284	  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
285	  const height = rng.int(p.heightMin, p.heightMax);    // Next(TreeHeightMin, TreeHeightMax+1)
286	  const num4 = height + p.topPadding;                  // + TreeTopPaddingNeeded
287	  // EmptyTileCheck(x-2, x+2, num-num4, num-1, 20)（cs:30580-30582）
288	  if (x - 2 < 0 || x + 2 >= st.w || num - num4 < 0) return false;
289	  for (let ex = x - 2; ex <= x + 2; ex++) {
290	    for (let ey = num - num4; ey <= num - 1; ey++) {
291	      const ei = st.idx(ex, ey);
292	      if (!st.flags[ei]) continue;
293	      if (!GEM_EMPTY_ALLOW.has(st.type[ei])) return false;
294	    }
295	  }
296	  // 干身帧循环（cs:30590-30816）：与 GrowTree 同表，tile 换成宝石树干
297	  let flagL = false, flagR = false;
298	  for (let i = num - height; i < num; i++) {
299	    // ★cs:30595-30596 掷序：先 num5=Next(3)（干身帧变体）**再** num6=Next(10)（枝型）。
300	    //   旧实现 num6 先 num5 后——两骰虽都无条件（总掷数同），但 num6 取自不同流位，
301	    //   枝型/重投序列整体漂移（shimmer 金标 2026-08-13 暴露：成树 x=3865 reroll 2 vs 1）。
302	    //   GrowTree（growTrunk）的顺序相反（枝型先），勿互搬。
303	    const num5 = rng.int(0, 2);
304	    let num6 = rng.int(0, 9);
305	    if (i === num - 1 || i === num - height) num6 = 0; // 顶/底强制直干
306	    while (((num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) && flagL) || ((num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) && flagR)) { num6 = rng.int(0, 9); }
307	    flagL = false; flagR = false;
308	    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) flagL = true;
309	    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) flagR = true;
310	    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num6][num5];
311	    st.setTileSilent(x, i, p.treeTile, fx, fy);
312	    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) {                    // 左枝（cs:30741-30764）
313	      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
314	      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [44, 198 + k * 22] : [66, k * 22];
315	      st.setTileSilent(x - 1, i, p.treeTile, bfx, bfy);
316	    }
317	    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) {                    // 右枝（cs:30766-30789）
318	      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
319	      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];
320	      st.setTileSilent(x + 1, i, p.treeTile, bfx, bfy);
321	    }
322	  }
323	  // 基座 tuft（cs:30819-30858）：两侧地面适树（草族）才出，各 1/3 概率抹除；
324	  // 以太腔地面是石头 → IsTileTypeFitForTree=false，天然全跳过（1:1 保留判定）
325	  const tuftFit = (dx: number) => {
326	    if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, num)) return false;
327	    const si = st.idx(x + dx, num);
328	    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && GEM_TREE_FIT.has(st.type[si]);
329	  };
330	  let tL = tuftFit(-1), tR = tuftFit(1);
331	  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) tL = false;
332	  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) tR = false;
333	  if (tR) st.setTileSilent(x + 1, num - 1, p.treeTile, 22, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
334	  if (tL) st.setTileSilent(x - 1, num - 1, p.treeTile, 44, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
335	  // ★cs:30906 `num5 = genRand.Next(3)` 是**无条件掷**（基座干身帧骰，帧写才受
336	  //   flag4/flag5 门控 cs:30907-30959）。旧实现内嵌 `if (tL || tR)`——两侧 tuft 全
337	  //   false 时（以太腔石地恒如此）每棵成树少掷 1 骰，500 采样流整体漂移
338	  //   （shimmer 金标 2026-08-13 暴露：CS oracle 状态恢复对账 tree[41] 后分叉）
339	  const baseK = rng.int(0, 2);
340	  if (tL && tR) st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, 88, 132 + baseK * 22);  // cs:30907-30923
341	  else if (tL) st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, 0, 132 + baseK * 22);    // cs:30925-30941
342	  else if (tR) st.setTileSilent(x, num - 1, p.treeTile, 66, 132 + baseK * 22);   // cs:30943-30959
343	  // 树冠标记帧（cs:30932-30967）：12/13 fx=22 否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k
344	  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;
345	  st.setTileSilent(x, num - height, p.treeTile, crownFx, 198 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
346	  return true;
347	}
348	
349	/** 宝石树（TryGrowingTreeByType 583-589 → GemTree_* profile）。
350	 *  treeTileType = 583-589（黄玉/紫晶/蓝玉/翡翠/红宝石/钻石/琥珀，cs:35063-35079 num24 映射） */
351	export function growGemTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, treeTileType: number): boolean {
352	  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, { ...GEM_PROFILE, treeTile: treeTileType });
353	}
354	
355	/** 观赏树（TryGrowingTreeByType 616 柳 / 596 樱 → VanityTree_Willow/Sakura profile，cs:3872-3894） */
356	export function growVanityTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, willow: boolean): boolean {
357	  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, willow ? WILLOW_PROFILE : SAKURA_PROFILE);
358	}
359	
360	/** 灰烬树（TryGrowingTreeByType 634 → GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.Tree_Ash，cs:3894-3903/
361	 *  30538）：灰烬草 633 基座、高 7-12。地狱 pass 铺 633 后 1/3 概率逐点尝试（cs:13885） */
362	export function growAshTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): boolean {
363	  return growTreeWithSettings(st, rng, x, y, ASH_PROFILE);
364	}
365	
366	/** AddTrees（1456 cs:31523-31578）1:1：逐列尝试（~1.6 列间距）；沙滩棕榈按每侧固定概率；
367	 *  草上 1/20 观赏树（type==2 且 Next(20)==0 → Next(2)==0 ? 柳 616 : 樱 596），否则普通树 5 */
368	export function runTreePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
369	  // EpicTree 林区（pass 82 头段，L9656-9666）：w*0.003 个 25-50 半宽林区逐格尝试
370	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w * 0.003; n++) {
371	    const cx = rng.int(50, st.w - 51);            // Next(50, maxTilesX-50)
372	    const hw = rng.int(25, 49);                  // Next(25, 50)
373	    for (let x = cx - hw; x < cx + hw; x++) {
374	      for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) growEpicTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
375	    }
376	  }
377	  const palmL = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;          // 每侧棕榈概率（L15644-45）
378	  const palmR = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;
379	  const GRASS_TILE = T.GRASS;                     // tile 2（vanilla 草）
380	  for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
381	    for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
382	      if (x < 380) {
383	        if (rng.next() < palmL && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
384	      } else if (x > st.w - 380) {
385	        if (rng.next() < palmR && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
386	      }
387	      // 1/20 观赏树（cs:31558-31566）：active 且 type==2 且 Next(20)==0 → 柳/樱对半
388	      const yi = st.idx(x, y);
389	      if (st.flags[yi] && st.type[yi] === GRASS_TILE && rng.int(0, 19) === 0) {
390	        growVanityTree(st, rng, x, y, rng.int(0, 1) === 0);
391	        continue;
392	      }
393	      growTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
394	    }
395	    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) x++;                 // Next(3)==0 跳列
396	    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) x++;                 // Next(4)==0 跳列
397	  }
398	}
399	
400	/** RandomizeTreeStyle（L2817）：按世界宽掷 treeX 边界与 treeStyle（互异，style0→4 概率 2/3）。
401	 *  Reset pass 调用（L4837，worldID 之后 dungeonSide 之前） */
402	export function randomizeTreeStyle(rng: RNG, w: number): { treeX: number[]; treeStyle: number[] } {
403	  const treeX = [0, 0, 0], treeStyle = [0, 0, 0, 0];
404	  const roll6 = () => rng.int(0, 5);
405	  // fix0 原位改写 treeStyle 前 count 位（cs:8095-8101 style0 → 4，2/3 概率）。
406	  // ★曾 fix0([treeStyle[0], treeStyle[1]]) 传入临时拷贝——掷骰照旧（Next(3) 无条件
407	  //   消耗）但翻转落在被丢弃的副本上，4200/6400 档 style0 从未被替换成 4
408	  //   （.wld 头 treeStyle 对账实锤，2026-08-16 多尺寸批修复）
409	  const fix0 = (arr: number[], count: number) => {
410	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

	public static bool TryGrowingTreeByType(int treeTileType, int checkedX, int checkedY, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)
	{
		bool result = false;
		switch (treeTileType)
		{
		case 5:
			result = GrowTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 323:
			result = GrowPalmTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 587:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Ruby, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 588:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Diamond, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 583:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Topaz, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 584:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amethyst, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 589:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amber, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);

		case 586:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Emerald, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);

		case 585:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Sapphire, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);

			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Sakura, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);

			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Willow, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);

		case 634:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.Tree_Ash, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	{

	public static bool EmptyTileCheck(int startX, int endX, int startY, int endY, int ignoreID = -1)
	{
		if (startX < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (endX >= Main.maxTilesX)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (startY < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (endY >= Main.maxTilesY)
		{
			return false;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		if (ignoreID != -1 && TileID.Sets.CommonSapling[ignoreID])
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		for (int i = startX; i < endX + 1; i++)
		{
			for (int j = startY; j < endY + 1; j++)
			{
				if (!Main.tile[i, j].active())
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch (ignoreID)
				{
				case -1:
					return false;
				case 11:
				{
					ushort type = Main.tile[i, j].type;
					if (type != 11)

					}
					continue;

				case 71:
				{
					ushort type = Main.tile[i, j].type;
					if (type != 71)
					{
						return false;
					}
					continue;

So vanilla can plant trees on slope-adjacent columns; ours additionally requires a neighboring full brick — on hilly terrain this could cut acceptance by a LOT. This is likely THE dominant bug.

	public static bool IsTileTypeFitForTree(ushort type)
	{
		switch (type)
		{
		case 2:
		case 23:
		case 60:
		case 70:
		case 109:
		case 147:
		case 199:
		case 477:
		case 492:
		case 633:
		case 661:
		case 662:
			return true;
		default:
			return false;
		}
	}

	public static bool IsTileATreeBranch(int x, int y, out int offsetToTrunk)
	{
		offsetToTrunk = 0;
		if (!InWorld(x, y, 2))
		{
			return false;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile == null || !tile.active() || tile.type == 323 || !TileID.Sets.IsATreeTrunk[tile.type])
		{
			return false;
		}
		if ((tile.frameX == 44 && (tile.frameY == 198 || tile.frameY == 220 || tile.frameY == 242)) || (tile.frameX == 66 && (tile.frameY == 0 || tile.frameY == 22 || tile.frameY == 44)))
		{
			offsetToTrunk = 1;
			return true;
		}
		if ((tile.frameX == 66 && (tile.frameY == 198 || tile.frameY == 220 || tile.frameY == 242)) || (tile.frameX == 88 && (tile.frameY == 66 || tile.frameY == 88 || tile.frameY == 110)))
		{
			offsetToTrunk = -1;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool IsTileATreeRoot(int x, int y, out int offsetToTrunk)
	{
		offsetToTrunk = 0;
		if (!InWorld(x, y, 2))
		{
			return false;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile == null || !tile.active() || tile.type == 323 || !TileID.Sets.IsATreeTrunk[tile.type])
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (tile.frameX == 44 && (tile.frameY == 132 || tile.frameY == 154 || tile.frameY == 176))
		{
			offsetToTrunk = 1;
			return true;
		}
		if (tile.frameX == 22 && (tile.frameY == 132 || tile.frameY == 154 || tile.frameY == 176))
		{
			offsetToTrunk = -1;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool GrowTree(int i, int y, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)

//     槽名由 onWorldPartial 回溯（管线重排鲁棒——槽名→pass 映射而非位置）；

// （默认 skip 防拖慢并行会话全量套件——同 _gem-dist-audit 的 SW_AUDIT 门）

import { describe, it } from 'vitest';

import { readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import type { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';

const VG = '/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g';
const W = 4200, H = 1200, N = W * H;

const SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET[i] = s;
}
SHEET[0] = 0;

const dumpFiles: { idx: number; base: string; name: string }[] = [];
for (const f of readdirSync(VG)) {
  const m = /^(\d{3})_(.+)\.typ$/.exec(f);
  if (m) dumpFiles.push({ idx: +m[1], base: `${VG}/${f.slice(0, -4)}`, name: m[2] });
}
dumpFiles.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);

const dumpCache = new Map<number, { typ: Uint16Array; act: Uint8Array; wal: Uint16Array; liq: Uint8Array; name: string }>();
function loadDump(idx: number) {
  if (idx < 0 || idx > 105) return undefined;
  let d = dumpCache.get(idx);
  if (d) return d;
  const e = dumpFiles.find((x) => x.idx === idx);
  if (!e) return undefined;
  const typB = readFileSync(`${e.base}.typ`), walB = readFileSync(`${e.base}.wal`);
  d = {
    typ: new Uint16Array(typB.buffer, typB.byteOffset, N),
    act: readFileSync(`${e.base}.act`),
    wal: new Uint16Array(walB.buffer, walB.byteOffset, N),
    liq: readFileSync(`${e.base}.liq`),
    name: e.name,
  };
  if (dumpCache.size > 6) dumpCache.clear();
  dumpCache.set(idx, d);
  return d;
}

// ---- 槽名（passes[] name）→ 该槽最后一个原版 pass 的 dump idx ----

h: number; act: Uint8Array; typ: Uint16Array; fx?: Uint16Array; fy?: Uint16Array }\n\nfunction loadDump(base: string): RawTiles {\n  const typ = new Uint16Array(readFileSync(`${base}.typ`).buffer, 0, N);\n  const act = readFileSync(`${base}.act`);\n  return { w: W, h: H, act, typ };\n}\n\n/** 干列检测：返回每棵树 {x, yBase}（yBase=干底行=base-1） */\nfunction trunkColumns(t: RawTiles, minRun = 5) {\n  const trees: { x: number; yBase: number; run: number }[] = [];\n  for (let x = 0; x < t.w; x++) {\n    let run = 0, runEnd = -1;\n    for (let y = 0; y <= t.h; y++) {\n      const on = y < t.h && t.act[idx(x, y)] === 1 && t.typ[idx(x, y)] === 5;\n      if (on) { run++; runEnd = y; }\n      else {\n        if (run >= minRun) trees.push({ x, yBase: runEnd, run });\n        run = 0;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return trees;\n}\n\n/** 帧级计数（.wld / 我们的 store）：冠层标记 (fx∈{0,22}, fy∈{198,220,242}) */\nfunction crownMarkers(t: RawTiles) {\n  const out: { x: number; y: number; typ: number }[] = [];\n  for (let y = 0; y < t.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < t.w; x++) {\n    const i = idx(x, y);\n    if (t.act[i] !== 1) continue;\n    const ty = t.typ[i];\n    if (ty !== 5) continue;\n    if ((t.fx![i] === 0 || t.fx![i] === 22) && (t.fy![i] === 198 || t.fy![i] === 220 || t.fy![i] === 242)) {\n      out.push({ x, y, typ: ty });\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction typeTileCount(t: RawTiles, type: number) {\n  let n = 0, cols = new Set<number>();\n  for (let y = 0; y < t.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < t.w; x++) {\n    const i = idx(x, y);\n    if (t.act[i] === 1 && t.typ[i] === type) { n++; cols.add(x); }\n  }\n  return { tiles: n, cols: cols.size };\n}\n\nfunction regionStats(trees: { x: number }[], treeX: number[], w: number) {\n  const bounds = [0, treeX[0], treeX[1], treeX[2], w];\n  const by = [0, 0, 0, 0];\n  for (const tr of trees) {\n    let r = 3;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) if (tr.x >= bounds[k] && tr.x < bounds[k + 1]) { r = k; break; }\n    by[r]++;\n  }\n  return by;\n}\n\nfunction worldToRaw(world: World): RawTiles {\n  const st = world.store;\n  return { w: st.w, h: st.h, act: st.flags, typ: st.type, fx: st.frameX, fy: st.frameY };\n}\n\nfunction wldToRaw(wld: ReturnType<typeof parseWld>): RawTiles {\n  const w = wld.tilesWide;\n  return { w, h: wld.tilesHigh, act: wld.tiles.active, typ: wld.tiles.type, fx: wld.tiles.frameX, fy: wld.tiles.frameY };\n}\n\ndescribe('树木数量大额差异专项', { timeout: 600_000 }, () => {\n  it('A/B：Trees pass 输出对账（9293480 evil=0 4200×1200）', async () => {\n    const van = loadDump('/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g/082_Planting_Trees');\n    const vanPre = loadDump('/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g/081_Sunflowers');\n    const vanTrees = trunkColumns(van);\n    const vanPreTrees = trunkColumns(vanPre);\n    console.log('[vanilla 082] 干列树数 =', vanTrees.length, '（pre=081:', vanPreTrees.length, '——pass 82 前已有 tile5 干列应为 0）');\n\n    let ours: World | null = null;\n    const world = await generateWorld({\n      seedText: '9293480', width: W, height: H, evil: 0,\n      onWorldPartial: (w, _i, name) => { if (name === '栽树') ours = w; },\n    });\n    // 拷贝「栽树」末态（后续 pass 会改 store）\n    const st = ours!.store;\n    const snap: RawTiles = {\n      w: W, h: H,\n      act: st.flags.slice(), typ: st.type.slice(), fx: st.frameX.slice(), fy: st.frameY.slice(),\n    };\n    const ourTrees = trunkColumns(snap);\n    const ourCrowns = crownMarkers(snap);\n    console.log('[ours 栽树末] 干列树数 =', ourTrees.length, ' 冠层标记 =', ourCrowns.length);\n\n    // 干列坐标集合对比\n    const vanSet = new Set(vanTrees.map(t => `${t.x},${t.yBase}`));\n    const ourSet = new Set(ourTrees.map(t => `${t.x},${t.yBase}`));\n    const miss = [...vanSet].filter(k => !ourSet.has(k));\n    const extra = [...ourSet].filter(k => !vanSet.has(k));\n    console.log(`[对账] 同坐标=${vanSet.size - miss.length} 原版独有(我们缺)=${miss.length} 我们独有=${extra.length}`);\n    if (miss.length) console.log('  缺失样本(前40):', miss.slice(0, 40).join(' '));\n    if (extra.length) console.log('  多出样本(前40):', extra.slice(0, 40).join(' '));\n\n    // 分区（treeX 从最终 world 取——我们的 treeX 与原版同掷链）\n    const tx = (world as any).treeX ?? [0, 0, 0];\n    console.log('[分区][原版]', regionStats(vanTrees, tx, W), '[我们]', regionStats(ourTrees, tx, W));\n\n    // 其他树族\n    for (const ty of [323, 191, 192, 583, 584, 585, 586, 587, 588, 589, 596, 616, 634]) {\n      const a = typeTileCount(van, ty), b = typeTileCount(snap, ty);\n      if (a.tiles || b.tiles) console.log(`  tile ${ty}: vanilla tiles=${a.tiles} cols=${a.cols} | ours tiles=${b.tiles} cols=${b.cols}`);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('C：最终 .wld vs JS 完整生成（12345 / 9293480）', async () => {\n    for (const [f, seedText] of [['12345世界', '12345'], ['9293480世界', '9293480']] as const) {\n      const wld = parseWld(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`tests/golden/wld/${f}.wld`)));\n      const raw = wldToRaw(wld);\n      const vanCrowns = crownMarkers(raw);\n      const vanTrunks = trunkColumns(raw);\n      console.log(`[${f}.wld ${wld.tilesWide}x${wld.tilesHigh} evil=${wld.isCrimson ? 1 : 0}] 冠层标记=${vanCrowns.length} 干列=${vanTrunks.length} treeX=${JSON.stringify(wld.treeX)} treeStyle=${JSON.stringify(wld.treeStyle)}`);\n      const world = await generateWorld({ seedText, width: wld.tilesWide, height: wld.tilesHigh, evil: wld.isCrimson ? 1 : 0 });\n      const ourRaw = worldToRaw(world);\n      const ourCrowns = crownMarkers(ourRaw);\n      const ourTrunks = trunkColumns(ourRaw);\n      console.log(`  [ours 最终] 冠层标记=${ourCrowns.length} 干列=${ourTrunks.length} treeX=${JSON.stringify((world as any).treeX)}`);\n      const vc = new Set(vanCrowns.map(c => `${c.x},${c.y}`)), oc = new Set(ourCrowns.map(c => `${c.x},${c.y}`));\n      const miss = [...vc].filter(k => !oc.has(k)), extra = [...oc].filter(k => !vc.has(k));\n      console.log(`  [对账] 同位=${vc.size - miss.length} 缺=${miss.length} 多=${extra.length}`);\n      const tx = wld.treeX;\n      console.log('  [分区][原版]', regionStats(vanTrunks, tx, wld.tilesWide), '[我们]', regionStats(ourTrunks, tx, wld.tilesWide));\n      // 树族 tiles\n      for (const ty of [323, 191, 192, 583, 584, 585, 586, 587, 588, 589, 596, 616, 634]) {\n        const a = typeTileCount(raw, ty), b = typeTileCount(ourRaw, ty);\n        if (a.tiles || b.tiles) console.log(`  tile ${ty}: vanilla tiles=${a.tiles} cols=${a.cols} | ours tiles=${b.tiles} cols=${b.cols}`);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n});\n"
}

 "content": "// 树木数量大额差异专项（2026-08-17）· 量化探针\n// 通道：\n//  A) 原版逐 pass dump（/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g，seed=9293480 evil=0 4200×1200）\n//     082_Planting_Trees = Trees pass 输出（无帧数组 → 干列算法数树）\n//  B) JS generateWorld 同种子/尺寸/evil，onWorldPartial 截「栽树」pass 末态\n//  C) 客户端金标最终 .wld（12345世界 / 9293480世界）帧级冠层标记数树 vs JS 完整生成最终态\n// 干列算法（帧无关，A/B 两侧同算法）：列内连续 type==5(vanilla id) 且 active ≥5 行 = 一棵树干\n//  （普通树高 5-16、Epic 20-29；侧枝/根单格不成 5 连）。\n// 注意：我们的 store.type 是内部 id 空间——经 TILE_DEFS[].vanilla.sheet 映射回原版 id。\n// 跑法：npx vitest run tools/_treeparity.test.ts\nimport { describe, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { parseWld } from '../src/wld/WldParser';\nimport { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';\nimport type { World } from '../src/world/World';\n\nconst SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);\nfor (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET[i] = s;\n}\n\ninterface RawTiles {\n  w: number; h: number;\n  act: Uint8Array; van: Int32Array;   // van = 原版 sheet id\n  fx?: Uint16Array; fy?: Uint16Array;\n}\nconst mk = (w: number, h: number): RawTiles => ({\n  w, h, act: new Uint8Array(w * h), van: new Int32Array(w * h).fill(-1),\n});\n\nfunction fromDump(base: string, w: number, h: number, n: number): RawTiles {\n  const t = mk(w, h);\n  const typ = new Uint16Array(readFileSync(`${base}.typ`).buffer, 0, n);\n  const act = readFileSync(`${base}.act`);\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { t.act[i] = act[i]; t.van[i] = typ[i]; }\n  return t;\n}\n\nfunction fromStore(world: World): RawTiles {\n  const st = world.store;\n  const t = mk(st.w, st.h);\n  for (let i = 0; i < st.w * st.h; i++) {\n    t.act[i] = st.flags[i] ? 1 : 0;\n    t.van[i] = SHEET[st.type[i]] ?? -1;\n  }\n  t.fx = st.frameX.slice(); t.fy = st.frameY.slice();\n  return t;\n}\n\nfunction fromWld(wld: ReturnType<typeof parseWld>): RawTiles {\n  const t = mk(wld.tilesWide, wld.tilesHigh);\n  const N = t.w * t.h;\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { t.act[i] = wld.tiles.active[i]; t.van[i] = wld.tiles.type[i]; }\n  t.fx = wld.tiles.frameX; t.fy = wld.tiles.frameY;\n  return t;\n}\n\n/** 干列检测：每棵树 {x, yBase}（yBase=干底行） */\nfunction trunkColumns(t: RawTiles, minRun = 5) {\n  const trees: { x: number; runEnd = y; } else { if (run >= minRun) trees.push({ x, yBase: runEnd, run }); run = 0; }\n    }\n  }\n  return trees;\n}\n\n/** 帧级冠层标记（需要 fx/fy） */\nfunction crownMarkers(t: RawTiles) {\n  const out: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];\n  for (let y = 0; y < t.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < t.w; x++) {\n    const i = y * t.w + x;\n    if (t.act[i] !== 1 || t.van[i] !== 5) continue;\n    if ((t.fx![i] === 0 || t.fx![i] === 22) && (t.fy![i] === 198 || t.fy![i] === 220 || t.fy![i] === 242)) out.push({ x, y });\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction typeStats(t: RawTiles, vanId: number) {\n  let tiles = 0; const cols = new Set<number>();\n  for (let y = 0; y < t.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < t.w; x++) {\n    const i = y * t.w + x;\n    if (t.act[i] === 1 && t.van[i] === vanId) { tiles++; cols.add(x); }\n  }\n  return { tiles, cols: cols.size };\n}\n\nfunction regionStats(trees: { x: number }[], treeX: number[], w: number) {\n  const b = [0, treeX[0], treeX[1], treeX[2], w];\n  const by = [0, 0, 0, 0];\n  for (const tr of trees) {\n    let r = 3;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) if (tr.x >= b[k] && tr.x < b[k + 1]) { r = k; break; }\n    by[r]++;\n  }\n  return by;\n}\n\nconst TREE_FAMILY = [323, 191, 192, 583, 584, 585, 586, 587, 588, 589, 596, 616, 634];\n\ndescribe('树木数量大额差异专项', { timeout: 600_000 }, () => {\n  it('AB：Trees pass 输出对账（9293480 evil=0 4200×1200）', async () => {\n    const W = 4200, H = 1200, N = W * H;\n    const van = fromDump('/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g/082_Planting_Trees', W, H, N);\n    const vanPre = fromDump('/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g/081_Sunflowers', W, H, N);\n    const vanTrees = trunkColumns(van);\n    console.log('[vanilla 082] 干列树数 =', vanTrees.length, '（081 前=, 应 0:', trunkColumns(vanPre).length, '）');\n\n    let snap: World | null = null;\n    const world = await generateWorld({\n      seedText: '9293480', width: W, height: H, evil: 0,\n      onWorldPartial: (w, _i, name) => { if (name === '栽树') snap = w; 1 : 0}] 冠层=${vanCrowns.length} 干列=${vanTrunks.length} treeX=${JSON.stringify(wld.treeX)} style=${JSON.stringify(wld.treeStyle)}`);\n      const world = await generateWorld({ seedText, width: wld.tilesWide, height: wld.tilesHigh, evil: wld.isCrimson ?  "content": "// 树木数量大额差异专项 · 归因探针（2026-08-17）\n// 在「向日葵」pass 末态（= Trees pass 输入，已验证与原版 081 dump 四数组一致）上，\n// 对每个潜在树基座格分别用【我们 TreePass 现行谓词】vs【原版 1456 谓词】评估\n// GrowTree 全部门（液体/基座/墙/侧格/净空盒），统计各门的通过率差——定位数量差根因。\n// 高度固定 h=10（净空盒 14；丛林草 +5）消除 RNG 高度差，只比谓词。\n// 跑法：npx vitest run tools/_treegate.test.ts\nimport { describe, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { idsByVanillaSheet } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/TileRunner';\nimport type { World } from '../src/world/World';\n\nconst PLANTS = idsByVanillaSheet(20);\nconst FIT_OURS = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 70, 109, 147, 199, 477, 492);            // TreePass.ts 现行 9 项\nconst FIT_VAN = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 70, 109, 147, 199, 477, 492, 633, 661, 662); // 1456 cs:29852 12 项\nconst WALLS_OURS = new Set(idsByVanillaSheet(0, 80, 106, 107, 138, 139, 140, 141, 145, 150, 152)); // 1405 旧表 11 项\nconst WALLS_VAN = new Set(idsByVanillaSheet(0, 150, 138, 145, 107, 152, 140, 139, 141, 106, 245, 315, 317, 63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 81, 70, 264, 268, 265, 74, 80)); // AllowsPlantsToGrow\n/** EmptyTileCheck ignoreID=20 容忍集（1456 cs:31611）：CommonSapling{20,590,595,615} ∪ 装饰表 */\nconst IGNORE_VAN = idsByVanillaSheet(20, 590, 595, 615, 3, 24, 32, 61, 62, 69, 71, 73, 74, 82, 83, 84, 110, 113, 184, 201, 233, 352, 485, 529, 530, 637, 655);\nconst JUNGLE = idsByVanillaSheet(60);\n\ninterface Ctx { st: World['store']; surface: number }\n\nfunction boxClear(ctx: Ctx, x: number, j: number, h: number, ignore: Set<number> | null): { ok: boolean; blocker: number } {\n  const { st } = ctx;\n  const clear = h + 4 + (JUNGLE.has(st.type[st.idx(x, j)]) ? 5 : 0);\n  const x0 = x - 2, x1 = x + 2, y0 = j - clear, y1 = j - 1;\n  if (x0 < 0 || x1 >= st.w || y0 < 0 || y1 >= st.h) return { ok: false, blocker: -2 };\n  for (let xx = x0; xx <= x1; xx++) for (let yy = y0; yy <= y1; yy++) {\n    const i = st.idx(xx, yy);\n    if (!st.flags[i]) continue;\n    if (ignore && ignore.has(st.type[i])) continue;\n    return { ok: false, blocker: st.type[i] };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, blocker: 0 };\n}\n\ndescribe('GrowTree 门谓词归因（9293480 evil=0 4200×1200）', { timeout: 300_000 }, () => {\n  it('逐门通过率对比', async () => {\n    let pre: World | null = null;\n    await generateWorld({\n      seedText: '9293480', width: 4200, height: 1200, evil: 0,\n      onWorldPartial: (w, _i, name) => { if (name === '向日葵') pre = w; },\n    });\n    const st = pre!.store;\n    const worldSurface = Math.floor((pre as unknown as { worldSurface: number }).worldSurface ?? 250);\n    const ctx: Ctx = { st, surface: worldSurface };\n\n    // 候选 = AddTrees 会把 GrowTree 带到 nactive+fit 门的所有格（含非表面格，\n    // 但液体/基座门会滤掉绝大多数；我们只在「基座门通过」集合上统计下游门）\n    let cand = 0;                     // 基座门通过（两边相同）\n    let liq = 0;\n    const wallOurs = { pass: 0, fail: 0 }, wallVan = { pass: 0, fail: 0 };\n    const sideOurs = { pass: 0, fail: 0 }, sideVan = { pass: 0, fail: 0 };\n    const boxOurs = { pass: 0, fail: 0 }, boxVan = { pass: 0, fail: 0 };\n    const allOurs = { pass: 0 }, allVan = { pass: 0 };\n    const boxBlockersOurs = new Map<number, number>(), boxBlockersVan = new Map<number, number>();\n    const wallFailIds = new Map<number, number>(), sideFailShape = { half: 0, slope: 0 };\n\n    for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {\n      for (let y = 20; y < worldSurface; y++) {\n        // GrowTree 头：越 plants（pass82 表面无 20，等价直落）\n        let j = y;\n        while (st.inBounds(x, j) && st.flags[st.idx(x, j)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(x, j)])) j++;\n        const i = st.idx(x, j);\n        if (!st.flags[i]) continue;\n        if (!(!st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0)) continue;       // nactive+整砖（两边同）\n        const fitOurs = FIT_OURS.has(st.type[i]), fitVan = FIT_VAN.has(st.type[i]);\n        if (!fitOurs && !fitVan) continue;\n        // 液体门（两边同）\n        if (st.liquid[st.idx(x - 1, j - 1)] > 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x, j - 1)] > 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(x + 1, j - 1)] > 0) { liq++; continue; }\n        cand++;\n\n        const w = st.wall[st.idx(x, j - 1)];\n        const wOKo = WALLS_OURS.has(w), wOKv = WALLS_VAN.has(w);\n        wOKo ? wallOurs.pass++ : wallOurs.fail++;\n        wOKv ? wallVan.pass++ : wallVan.fail++;\n        if (!wOKv && wOKo) wallFailIds.set(w, (wallFailIds.get(w) ?? 0) + 1);\n\n        const sideOK = (dx: number, fit: Set<number>, strict: boolean) => {\n          if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, j)) return false;\n          const si = st.idx(x + dx, j);\n          if (!st.flags[si] || !fit.has(st.type[si])) return false;\n          if (strict && (st.half[si] || st.slope[si] !== 0)) return false;\n          return true;\n        };\n        const sOKo = sideOK(-1, FIT_OURS, true) || sideOK(1, FIT_OURS, true);\n        const sOKv = sideOK(-1, FIT_VAN, false) || sideOK(1, FIT_VAN, false);\n        sOKo ? sideOurs.pass++ : sideOurs.fail++;\n        sOKv ? sideVan.pass++ : sideVan.fail++;\n        if (sOKv && !sOKo) {\n          for (const dx of [-1, 1]) {\n            const si = st.idx(x + dx, j);\n            if (st.flags[si] && FIT_VAN.has(st.type[si])) { if (st.half[si]) sideFailShape.half++; else if (st.slope[si] !== 0) sideFailShape.slope++; }\n          }\n        }\n\n        const bo = boxClear(ctx, x, j, 10, null);\n        const bv = boxClear(ctx, x, j, 10, IGNORE_VAN);\n        bo.ok ? boxOurs.pass++ : (boxOurs.fail++, boxBlockersOurs.set(bo.blocker, (boxBlockersOurs.get(bo.blocker) ?? 0) + 1));\n        bv.ok ? boxVan.pass++ : (boxVan.fail++, boxBlockersVan.set(bv.blocker, (boxBlockersVan.get(bv.blocker) ?? 0) + 1));\n\n        if (wOKo && sOKo && bo.ok) allOurs.pass++;\n        if (wOKv && sOKv && bv.ok) allVan.pass++;\n      }\n    }\n\n    const mkSheet = (m: Map<number, number>) => [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8)\n      .map(([k, v]) => `${k}:${v}`).join(' ');\n    console.log(`候选基座格(基座门+液体门通过)=${cand}（液体拒绝另 ${liq}）`);\n    console.log(`墙门: ours通过=${wallOurs.pass} vanilla通过=${wallVan.pass}（ ours多拒 ${wallOurs.fail - wallVan.fail}——墙id分布 ${mkSheet(wallFailIds)}）`);\n    console.log(`侧格门: ours=${sideOurs.pass} vanilla=${sideVan.pass}（ours多拒 ${sideVan.pass - sideOurs.pass}，其中半砖 ${sideFailShape.half} 坡面 ${sideFailShape.slope} 计数含双邻）`);\n    console.log(`净空盒(h=10): ours=${boxOurs.pass} vanilla=${boxVan.pass}（ours多拒 ${boxVan.pass - boxOurs.pass}）`);\n    console.log(`  ours盒挡路tile: ${mkSheet(boxBlockersOurs)}`);\n    console.log(`  van 盒挡路tile: ${mkSheet(boxBlockersVan)}`);\n    console.log(`全门连乘: ours=${allOurs.pass} vanilla=${allVan.pass} → 谓词差致缺 ${(allVan.pass / Math.max(1, allOurs.pass)).toFixed(2)}x`);\n  });\n});\n"
}
```

```
Type URType = asm.GetType("Terraria.Utilities.UnifiedRandom")!;
void Replay() => r = (dynamic)Activator.CreateInstance(URType, seed)!;


// ---- 网格（vanilla tile id；行主序 y*W+x，同 JS TileStore）----
var act = new byte[W * H];
var typ = new ushort[W * H];
var wal = new ushort[W * H];
var liq = new byte[W * H];
var liqT = new byte[W * H];
var checkpoints = new List<string>();

static byte[] ToBytes(ushort[] a) { var b = new byte[a.Length * 2]; Buffer.BlockCopy(a, 0, b, 0, b.Length); return b; }
uint Fnv16(ushort[] a) { uint h = 0x811c9dc5; foreach (var v in a) { h = (h ^ (uint)(v & 0xff)) * 16777619u; h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 8) & 0xff)) * 16777619u; } return h; }
static uint Fnv8(byte[] a) { uint h = 0x811c9dc5; foreach (var v in a) { h = (h ^ v) * 16777619u; h = h * 16777619u; } return h; }
// 只比活性 type（非活性格的幽灵 type 归零——与 JS 侧 hashGrids 口径一致；
// 幽灵 parity 是独立对账项：原版幽灵会存盘、我们 finalize 清除，玩法无差）
var hashTyp = new ushort[W * H];
var hashGho = new ushort[W * H];
var ghostPts = new List<string>();
void Checkpoint(string name)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < typ.Length; i++) {
        hashTyp[i] = (ushort)(act[i] != 0 ? typ[i] : 0);
        hashGho[i] = (ushort)(act[i] == 0 ? typ[i] : 0); // inactive 格残留 type（幽灵 parity）
    }
    checkpoints.Add($"{name}|{Fnv16(hashTyp):x8} {Fnv8(act):x8} {Fnv16(wal):x8} {Fnv8(liq):x8}");
    if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("STREAM_ALL") != null) Console.WriteLine("STREAM " + name + "|" + StreamHash().ToString("x8"));
    var _da = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("DUMP_ALL_DIR");
    if (_da != null) {
        File.WriteAllBytes(_da + "/" + name + ".typ", ToBytes(typ));
        File.WriteAllBytes(_da + "/" + name + ".act", act);
        File.WriteAllBytes(_da + "/" + name + ".wal", ToBytes(wal));
        File.WriteAllBytes(_da + "/" + name + ".liq", liq);
    }

    ghostPts.Add($"{name}|{Fnv16(hashGho):x8}");
    // GRID_DUMP=pyramids:/tmp/cs-pyr → 命中检查点时落 typ(LE16)/act/wal(LE16)/liq 裸数组（对拍用）
    var gd = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("GRID_DUMP");
    if (gd != null && gd.Split(':')[0] == name)
    {
        string p = gd.Split(':')[1];
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".typ", ToBytes(typ));
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".act", act);
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".wal", ToBytes(wal));
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".liq", liq);
        Console.WriteLine($"griddump {name} -> {p}");
    }
    // STATE_DUMP=dirtwallcleanup:/tmp/cs-st39 → 同上 + UnifiedRandom 内部态（结构段状态恢复对拍：
    // 上游 pass 多代理并发修改期，从该态直跑 #40-44 裁决，等价全链且不受上游 WIP 影响）
    var sd = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("STATE_DUMP");

```

{

 "old_string": "/** EmptyTileCheck（L15685，ignoreID=-1 简化：出界 false，任何实心 false） */\nfunction emptyTileCheck(st: TileStore, x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number): boolean {\n  if (x0 < 0 || x1 >= st.w || y0 < 0 || y1 >= st.h) return false;\n  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}",
 "new_string": "/** CommonSapling（TileID.cs:173）：{20,590,595,615}——EmptyTileCheck 的\n *  ignoreID 语义核心：ignoreID ∈ 该集时，盒内同类树苗格全容忍 */\nconst COMMON_SAPLING = idsByVanillaSheet(20, 590, 595, 615);\n/** EmptyTileCheck ignoreID∈CommonSapling 时的额外装饰容忍表（1456 cs:31653-31665）：\n *  3 杂草/24 邪恶草/32/61 丛林草丛/62 丛林藤/69/73/74/82/83/84/110 神圣草丛/113/184/201/\n *  233 丛林大株/352/485 倒木/529/530/637/655——树苗门下这些装饰格不算占位 */\nconst EMPTY_DECOR = idsByVanillaSheet(3, 24, 32, 61, 62, 69, 71, 73, 74, 82, 83, 84, 110, 113, 184, 201, 233, 352, 485, 529, 530, 637, 655);\n\n/** EmptyTileCheck（1456 cs:31611-31683）1:1：出界 false；活跃格按 ignoreID 分派——\n *  -1：任何活跃格 false；11/71：仅容忍同 id；∈CommonSapling（GrowTree/GrowEpicTree/\n *  GrowPalmTree/GrowTreeWithSettings 全部传 20）：容忍树苗族 + 装饰表。\n *  ★旧实现 ignoreID=-1 简化（任何实心 false）——表面杂草(3)/倒木(485)等把净空盒\n *  全部判死，普通树数量差的主根因之一（vanilla 161 vs 我们 62，seed 9293480） */\nfunction emptyTileCheck(st: TileStore, x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number, ignoreID = -1): boolean {\n  if (x0 < 0 || x1 >= st.w || y0 < 0 || y1 >= st.h) return false;\n  const saplingMode = ignoreID !== -1 && COMMON_SAPLING.has(ignoreID);\n  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;\n      const t = st.type[i];\n      if (ignoreID === -1) return false;\n      if (ignoreID === 11) { if (t !== ignoreID) return false; continue; }\n      if (ignoreID === 71) { if (t !== ignoreID) return false; continue; }\n      if (saplingMode) {\n        if (COMMON_SAPLING.has(t)) continue;\n        if (EMPTY_DECOR.has(t)) continue;\n        return false;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}"
}

[66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];\n      st.setTileSilent(i + 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);\n    }\n  }\n  // 基座方向 num7（L14439-14543）：两侧地面适配 → 决定根部与基座帧。\n  //  侧根放置前提 = 邻格整砖(L30230/30234:!halfBrick && slope==0)——半砖/坡面侧不出根\n  const fitSide = (dx: number) => {\n    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;\n    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);\n    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);\n  };\n  let num7 = rng.int(0, 2);\n  const sideL = fitSide(-1), sideR = fitSide(1);\n  if (!sideL) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 2; else if (num7 === 1) num7 = 3; }\n  if (!sideR) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 1; else if (num7 === 2) num7 = 3; }\n  if (sideL && !sideR) num7 = 2;\n  if (sideR && !sideL) num7 = 1;\n  const k8 = rng.int(0, 2);\n  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 1) st.setTileSilent(i + 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 22, 132 + k8 * 22); // 右根\n  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 2) st.setTileSilent(i - 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 44, 132 + k8 * 22); // 左根\n  // 基座干身（switch num7，L14519-14566）\n  const baseFx = num7 === 0 ? 88 : num7 === 1 ? 0 : num7 === 2 ? 66 : -1;\n  if (baseFx >= 0) st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, TREE, baseFx, 132 + k8 * 22);\n  // 树冠标记帧（L14568-14600）：12/13 fx=22，否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k\n  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;\n  st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - height, TREE, crownFx, 198 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);\n  gs.genTrees.push({ x: i, y: index1 - height, h: height }); // 砍伐整棵判定用\n  return true;\n}",
 "new_string": "/** 树干+枝+根+冠 共体（GrowTree 1456 cs:29983-30395 / GrowEpicTree cs:27405-27760 同表）。\n *  groundType：基座 tile（判丛林加高）；返回是否成树。\n *  ★cs:29983-29986 掷序：先 num4=Next(3)（干身变体）**再** num5=Next(10)（枝型），\n *    强制直干/重投只作用在 num5——旧实现枝型先掷致流值互换（1405 反编译的\n *    frameNumber 位已废弃，1456 无该掷）。growTreeWithSettings 掷序相同（cs:30595）。 */\nfunction growTrunk(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, index1: number, height: number): boolean {\n  let flagL = false, flagR = false; // 侧枝去重（num5 5/7 左、6/7 右不同帧连续限制）\n  for (let y = index1 - height; y < index1; y++) {\n    const num4 = rng.int(0, 2);                     // 干身变体（Next(3)，先掷）\n    let num5 = rng.int(0, 9);                       // 枝型 0-9（Next(10)，后掷）\n    if (y === index1 - 1 || y === index1 - height) num5 = 0;  // 顶/底强制直干\n    while (((num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) && flagL) || ((num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) && flagR)) {\n      num5 = rng.int(0, 9);\n    }\n    flagL = false; flagR = false;\n    if (num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) flagL = true;\n    if (num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) flagR = true;\n    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num5][num4];\n    st.setTileSilent(i, y, TREE, fx, fy);\n    // 侧枝帧（cs:30201-30247）：变体骰先掷，Next(3)<2 走 198+ 行（贴干枝）\n    if (num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) {\n      const k = rng.int(0, 2);\n      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [44, 198 + k * 22] : [66, k * 22];\n      st.setTileSilent(i - 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);\n    }\n    if (num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) {\n      const k = rng.int(0, 2);\n      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];\n      st.setTileSilent(i + 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);\n    }\n  }\n  // 基座方向 num6（cs:30248-30272）：先掷 Next(3)，再按两侧整砖适配翻转。\n  //  侧根放置前提 = 邻格 nactive 且整砖（cs:30251-30258）——半砖/坡面侧不出根\n  const fitSide = (dx: number) => {\n    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;\n    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);\n    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);\n  };\n  let num7 = rng.int(0, 2);\n  const sideL = fitSide(-1), sideR = fitSide(1);\n  if (!sideL) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 2; else if (num7 === 1) num7 = 3; }\n  if (!sideR) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 1; else if (num7 === 2) num7 = 3; }\n  if (sideL && !sideR) num7 = 2;\n  if (sideR && !sideL) num7 = 1;\n  // ★根部掷骰结构（cs:30273-30323）：右根/左根各自条件内独立掷，基座骰无条件掷\n  //  （num6==3 时基座无帧写但仍耗掷）——旧实现三处共用 1 骰，每棵成树少耗 1-2 骰，\n  //  流位漂移殃及后续树的接拒判定\n  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 1) st.setTileSilent(i + 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 22, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22); // 右根\n  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 2) st.setTileSilent(i - 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 44, 132 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22); // 左根\n  // 基座干身（switch num6，cs:30326-30371；num6==3 无写但掷骰照耗）\n  const baseFx = num7 === 0 ? 88 : num7 === 1 ? 0 : num7 === 2 ? 66 : -1;\n  const k8 = rng.int(0, 2);\n  if (baseFx >= 0) st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, TREE, baseFx, 132 + k8 * 22);\n  // 树冠标记帧（cs:30372-30395）：Next(13)!=0 → fx=22，否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k\n  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;\n  st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - height, TREE, crownFx, 198 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);\n  gs.genTrees.push({ x: i, y: index1 - height, h: height }); // 砍伐整棵判定用\n  return true;\n}"
}

 "new_string": "/** GrowEpicTree（1456 cs:27361-27760）：巨型树。基座严格草(2)、邻格草族、\n *  净空 55（ignoreID=20）、height=Next(20,30) */\nfunction growEpicTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, y: number): boolean {\n  let index1 = y;\n  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;\n  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;\n  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);\n  const GRASS = idsByVanillaSheet(2);\n  // 基座整砖检查(GrowEpicTree 同 GrowTree 头部语义:半砖/坡面拒绝)\n  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !GRASS.has(st.type[gi])) return false;\n  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;\n  const family = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 109);\n  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)\n    && st.flags[st.idx(i + dx, index1)] && family.has(st.type[st.idx(i + dx, index1)]);\n  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;\n  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - 55, index1 - 1, 20)) return false;\n  return growTrunk(st, rng, gs, i, index1, rng.int(20, 29)); // Next(20,30)\n}"

 "new_string": "/** GrowPalmTree（1456 cs:27260-27359）：沙滩棕榈，干身倾斜帧 fy=num4（±2 步进趋向 num3） */\n/** 导出供 StructuresPass 绿洲消费段复用(2026-08-13 去重) */\nexport function growPalmTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(i, y)) return false;\n  let index1 = y;\n  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;\n  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;\n  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);\n  // 基座整砖检查(cs:27282-27286 active && !halfBrick && slope==0)\n  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0) return false;\n  // ★墙门（cs:27288-27292）：DefaultTreeWallTest(AllowsPlantsToGrow 27 项)——\n  //  1405 是 wall==0；1456 放宽。液体门只查 num-1 一格\n  if (!TREE_WALLS.has(st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)]) || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;\n  if (!PALM_GROUND.has(st.type[gi])) return false;\n  // 净空盒两段（cs:27296-27304）：中柱 [i,i]×[num-2,num-1] + 侧柱 [i-1,i+1]×[num-20,num-3]，\n  // 均 ignoreID=20（1405 无 ignore 语义→杂草/倒木误拒）\n  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i, i, index1 - 2, index1 - 1, 20)) return false;\n  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 1, i + 1, index1 - 20, index1 - 3, 20)) return false;"

The growTreeWithSettings comment at line 343-346 claims "GrowTree（growTrunk）的顺序相反（枝型先）" — that claim was WRONG (I verified 1456 GrowTree rolls num4=Next(3) variant first). Both use variant-first. Fix the comment and unify the empty check. Note the empty check in growTreeWithSettings: replace the manual loop with emptyTileCheck(..., 20) which also adds CommonSapling tolerance.

Also the skip-loop quirk: vanilla `while (Main.tile[checkedX, checkedY].active() && Main.tile[checkedX, num].type == settings.SaplingTileType) num++;` — active read at ORIGINAL checkedY, not num. Ours reads flags at num. To be exact, mirror it.

} else { if (run >= 5) trees.push(`${x},${runEnd}`); run = 0; }\n    }\n  }\n  return trees;\n}\n\ndescribe('Trees 隔离重放（dump081 → TreePass vs dump082）', { timeout: 300_000 }, () => {\n  it('两变体 half/slope 夹逼', async () => {\n    // 拿我们「向日葵」末态的 half/slope 代理 + groundLevel\n    let proxyHalf: Uint8Array | null = null, proxySlope: Uint8Array | null = null;\n    let ground = 0;\n    await generateWorld({\n      seedText: '9293480', width: W, height: H, evil: 0,\n      onWorldPartial: (w, _i, name) => {\n        if (name === '向日葵') {\n          proxyHalf = w.store.half.slice(); st.liquid.set(liq);\n      const rng = new RNG(9293480);\n      const gs = { worldSurface: ground, genTrees: [] } as unknown as GenState;\n      runTreePass(st, rng, gs);\n      const ourTrees = storeTrees(st, 5);\n      const ourSet = new Set(ourTrees);\n      const vanByCol = new Map<number, string[]>();\n      for (const c of vanTrees5) { const x = +c.split(',')[0]; (vanByCol.get(x) ?? vanByCol.set(x, []).get(x)!).push(c); }\n      // 干列坐标对齐：vanilla 增量格是\"格集合\"——按列聚合出干底（列内最大 y）\n      const vanTrunkByCol = new Map<number, number>();\n      for (const c of vanTrees5) { const [x, y] = c.split(',').map(Number); vanTrunkByCol.set(x, Math.max(vanTrunkByCol.get(x) ?? 0, y)); }\n      const vanColSet = new Set(vanTrunkByCol.keys());\n      const ourCols = new Set(ourTrees.map(t => +t.split(',')[0]));\n      let same = 0, near = 0;\n      for (const x of ourCols) {\n        if (vanColSet.has(x)) same++;\n        else {\n          let n = false;\n          for (let d = -4; d <= 4; d++) if (d !== 0 && vanColSet.has(x + d)) { n = true; break; }\n          if (n) near++;\n        }\n      }\n      let palmpalm = 0;\n      for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) { let has = false; for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) { const i = y * W + x; if (st.flags[i] && SHEET[st.type[i]] === 323) { has = true; break; } } if (has) palmpalm++; }\n      console.log(`[${label}] 干列树=${ourSet.size}（vanilla 干列=${vanColSet.size}）同列=${same} 邻近±4=${near} 棕榈列=${palmpalm}（vanilla≈${new Set([...vanPalms].map(c => +c.split(',')[0])).size}）`);\n      const missCols = [...vanColSet].filter(x => !ourCols.has(x));\n      console.log(`   vanilla 独有列=${missCols.length} 样本: ${missCols.slice(0, 15).join(',')}`);\n    }\n  });\n});\n"

// 原版矿石 pass 27 "Shinies"（1405 WorldGen.cs:5837-5922；1456 OresAndShinies:13233 常数一致）
// + 宝石 pass 36 "Gems"（1405 L6754-6830，含尾部浮沙拱形化循环）。
// 密度=面积×常数、深度三带（surfLow..surfHigh / surfHigh..rockHigh / rockLow..底）、
// 脉体=TileRunner（菱形椭圆），邪恶矿 22/204 只在岩层以下。
// RNG 映射：C# Next(a,b) 左闭右开 → rng.int(a, b-1)。
import { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
import type { GenState } from './GenState';
import { tileRunner } from './TileRunner';
import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
import { flipGenSolid } from './GenSolid';

/** 单深度带放矿：count 次尝试，y∈[yMin,yMax)，strength/steps 各自区间 */
function band(
  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
  count: number, yMin: number, yMax: number,
  type: number, s: [number, number], steps: [number, number],
) {
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const x = rng.int(0, st.w - 1);              // Next(0, maxTilesX)
    const y = rng.int(Math.max(0, yMin), yMax - 1); // Next(yMin, yMax)
    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, {
      strength: rng.int(s[0], s[1]), steps: rng.int(steps[0], steps[1]),
      type, addTile: false,
    });
  }
}

const _origInt = null;
export function runShiniesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  const area = st.w * st.h;
  const surfLow = gs.worldSurfaceLow, surfHigh = gs.worldSurfaceHigh;
  const rockHigh = gs.rockLevelHigh, rockLow = gs.rockLevelLow;

  // 铜/锡：三带 6e-5 / 8e-5 / 2e-4（cs:5840-5862）
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 6e-5), surfLow, surfHigh, gs.oreTiers.copper, [3, 5], [2, 5]);
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 8e-5), surfHigh, rockHigh, gs.oreTiers.copper, [3, 6], [3, 6]);
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 2e-4), rockLow, st.h, gs.oreTiers.copper, [4, 8], [4, 7]);
  // 铁/铅：三带 3e-5 / 8e-5 / 2e-4（cs:5863-5892）
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 3e-5), surfLow, surfHigh, gs.oreTiers.iron, [3, 6], [2, 4]);
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 8e-5), surfHigh, rockHigh, gs.oreTiers.iron, [3, 5], [3, 5]);
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 2e-4), rockLow, st.h, gs.oreTiers.iron, [4, 8], [4, 7]);
  // 银/钨：中层 2.6e-5 / 深层 1.5e-4（cs:13548-13562）；天空带 1.7e-4 在金深层**之后**（cs:13585）
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 2.6e-5), surfHigh, rockHigh, gs.oreTiers.silver, [3, 5], [3, 5]);
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 1.5e-4), rockLow, st.h, gs.oreTiers.silver, [4, 8], [4, 7]);
  // 金/铂：深层 1.2e-4（cs:13563-13577）——注册序在银天空带之前（曾倒置致流分叉）
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 1.2e-4), rockLow, st.h, gs.oreTiers.gold, [4, 7], [4, 7]);
  // 银/钨天空带 1.7e-4（cs:13585-13598）/ 金近地表带 1.2e-4（cs:13599-13612）
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 1.7e-4), 0, surfLow, gs.oreTiers.silver, [4, 8], [4, 7]);
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 1.2e-4), 0, Math.max(1, surfLow - 20), gs.oreTiers.gold, [4, 7], [4, 7]);
  // 邪恶矿：2.25e-5，仅岩层以下（cs:5932-5949）
  const evil = gs.crimson ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_crimtane']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_demonite']!;
  band(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(area * 2.25e-5), gs.rockLevel, st.h, evil, [3, 5], [4, 7]);
}

/** 宝石 pass 36（cs:6754-6814）：六宝石独立数量（w×[0.3,0.1,0.25,0.45,0.5,0.05]×0.2），
 *  拒绝采样到石头上再 TileRunner；type 63-68 = 紫晶/黄玉/蓝宝/绿宝/红宝/钻石。 */
export function runGemsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  // ★pass 头 tileSolid 翻转（cs:15114 `Main.tileSolid[484] = false`）——DesertBiome
  //   头（cs:12537）已翻过,此处为原版冗余重放,接线仅为时间线存档（484 生成期恒非实心）。
  flipGenSolid([484], false);
  const gems: Array<[string, number]> = [
    ['gem_sapphire', 0.3],   // 63
    ['gem_ruby', 0.1],       // 64
    ['gem_emerald', 0.25],   // 65
    ['gem_topaz', 0.45],     // 66
    ['gem_amethyst', 0.5],   // 67
    ['gem_diamond', 0.05],   // 68
  ];
  for (const [key, density] of gems) {
    const type = TILE_BY_KEY[key]!;
    const count = Math.floor(st.w * density * 0.2);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      // cs:15144-15152:do-while 至多 3 轮采样(num2 从 3 递减,命中即停);
      // 3 轮全 miss 则 num2==0 跳过 TileRunner。命中判定 = active() && type==1
      let tries = 3, x = 0, y = 0, hit = false;
      do {
        x = rng.int(0, st.w - 1);
        y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 1);
        const ii = st.idx(x, y);
        hit = st.flags[ii] !== 0 && st.type[ii] === T.STONE;
      } while (!hit && --tries > 0);
      if (tries !== 0) {
        tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, {
          strength: rng.int(2, 5), steps: rng.int(3, 6), // Next(2,6)/Next(3,7)
          type, addTile: false,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  sandArch(st, gs);
}

    const x1 = dir === 0 ? st.w - 5 : 5;
    for (let x = x0; x !== x1; x += step) {
      if (ud && x > ud.x0 && x < ud.x1) continue;   // cs:15172 列级跳过（严格不等=界列仍处理）

function shimmerChain(seedText: string, evil: 0 | 1) {
  const { world, rng, gs, seed } = runResetAndTerrain({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText, evil });

  const replay = () => rng.reseed(seed);

  grids.set('terrain', hashGrids(st)); streams.set('terrain', streamHash(rng));

  runSandPatchesPass(st, rng, gs); snap('sandpatches');

  runTunnelsPass(st, rng, gs); snap('tunnels');

**陷阱实录**（已写进注释与报告）：ignoreID 形参 20 是原版 id，直接查内部 id 集 → saplingMode 恒 false → 盒检查全容忍 → 树爆炸 8×（1288 棵）；形参用原版 id 集、格内比较用内部 id 集修复。
